         // rows per block\n    size_t nb;         // number of global blocks\n    int rank;\n    int nprocs;\n    size_t localRows;  // number of rows stored on this rank\n\n    int owner(size_t g) const { return static_cast<int>(g % nprocs); }\n    size_t blockStart(size_t g) const { return g * B; }\n    size_t blockRows(size_t g) const { return std::min(B, n - g * B); }\n    // local row offset of a block owned by this rank\n    size_t localOffset(size_t g) const { return (g / nprocs) * B; }\n};\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const Distribution& d,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n\n    // Replay the sequential random stream, keeping only rows owned by this rank.\n    for (size_t g = 0; r < d.blockRows(g); ++r) {\n                out[r * n + d.blockStart(g) + r] = 0;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (size_t e = 0; e < count; ++e) {\n                (void)rand_r(&seed);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const Distribution& d) {\n    // path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    for (size_t g = d.rank; j < n; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dik + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = dRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        dRow[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        pRow[j] = better ? k : pRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply pivots k0..k0+nk-1 (pivot rows in buf) in order to rows [row0, row0+rows) of\n// the local matrices. Per element this is exactly the classic k-ordered update.\nstatic void applyPanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const unsigned int* buf,\n                       const size_t k0, const size_t nk, const size_t row0,\n                       const size_t rows, const size_t n, MPI_Request* pending) {\n    for (size_t r = row0; r < row0 + rows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* dRow = dist + r * n;\n        unsigned int* pRow = path + r * n;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n            relaxRow(dRow, pRow, buf + kk * n, dRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), n);\n        }\n        if (pending && *pending != MPI_REQUEST_NULL) {\n            int flag;\n            MPI_Test(pending, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);  // drive progress\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Owner of block g runs the classic algorithm over pivots of block g restricted to\n// the block's own rows, recording each pivot row at its iteration into buf.\nstatic void computePanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, unsigned int* buf,\n                         const Distribution& d, const size_t g) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n    const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n    const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n        const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n        unsigned int* rowK = buf + kk * n;\n        std::memcpy(rowK, dist + (off + kk) * n, n * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < nk; ++r) {\n            unsigned int* dRow = dist + (off + r) * n;\n            relaxRow(dRow, path + (off + r) * n, rowK, dRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), n);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const Distribution& d) {\n    // Blocked-pivot Floyd-Warshall over a block-cyclic row distribution.\n    // Pivot rows k are unchanged during iteration k (non-negative weights, zero\n    // diagonal), so each pivot block is computed by its owner and broadcast; the\n    // next panel is computed early and its broadcast overlapped with local updates.\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t B = d.B;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> bufs[2] = {std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n),\n                                         std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n)};\n    MPI_Request reqs[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    auto startBcast = [&](size_t g) {\n        unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        if (d.owner(g) == d.rank) {\n            computePanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, d, g);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf, static_cast<int>(d.blockRows(g) * n), MPI_UNSIGNED, d.owner(g),\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[g & 1]);\n    };\n\n    if (d.nb > 0) startBcast(0);\n\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        MPI_Wait(&reqs[g & 1], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n        const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n        const bool haveNext = g + 1 < d.nb;\n        const bool ownNext = haveNext && d.owner(g + 1) == d.rank;\n\n        if (haveNext) {\n            if (ownNext) {\n                applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(g + 1),\n                           d.blockRows(g + 1), n, nullptr);\n            }\n            startBcast(g + 1);\n        }\n\n        MPI_Request* pending = haveNext ? &reqs[(g + 1) & 1] : nullptr;\n        for (size_t h = d.rank; h < d.nb; h += d.nprocs) {\n            if (h == g || (ownNext && h == g + 1)) continue;\n            applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(h),\n                       d.blockRows(h), n, pending);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Collect the distributed matrix on rank 0 (full matrix returned on rank 0 only).\nstd::vector<unsigned int> gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,\n                                       const Distribution& d) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> full;\n    if (d.rank == 0) full.resize(n * n);\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        const int src = d.owner(g);\n        const size_t count = d.blockRows(g) * n;\n        if (d.rank == 0) {\n            unsigned int* dst = full.data() + d.blockStart(g) * n;\n            if (src == 0) {\n                std::memcpy(dst, local.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n, count * sizeof(unsigned int));\n            } else {\n                MPI_Recv(dst, static_cast<int>(count), MPI_UNSIGNED, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            }\n        } else if (src == d.rank) {\n            MPI_Send(local.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n, static_cast<int>(count), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                     0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    return full;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nmstart=src.index('int main(')\nmain=r'''int main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n    const bool root = rank == 0;\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Choose pivot block size: large enough for cache reuse, small enough that\n    // there are several blocks per rank for load balance and a short critical path.\n    Distribution d;\n    d.n = numNodes;\n    d.rank  floydWarshall(dist, path, d);\n    \n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate operations per printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n    if (printResults || validate) fullDist = gatherMatrix(dist, d);\n    \n    // Print results for printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, }\n    size_t blockStart(size_t g) const { return g * B; }\n    size_t blockRows(size_t g) const { return std::min(B, n - g * B); }\n    // local row offset of a block owned by this rank\n    size_t localOffset(size_t g) const { return (g / nprocs) * B;          // rows per block\n    size_t nb;         // number of global blocks\n    int rank;\n    int nprocs;\n    size_t localRows;  // number of rows stored on this rank\n\n    int owner(size_t g) const { return static_cast<int>(g % nprocs); }\n    size_t blockStart(size_t g) const { return g * B; }\n    size_t blockRows(size_t g) const { return std::min(B, n - g * B); }\n    // local row offset of a block owned by this rank\n    size_t localOffset(size_t g) const { return (g / nprocs) * B; }\n};\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const Distribution& d,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n\n    // Replay the sequential random stream, keeping only rows owned by this rank.\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        const size_t count = d.blockRows(g) * n;\n        if (d.owner(g) == d.rank) {\n            unsigned int* out = dist.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n;\n            for (size_t e = 0; e < count; ++e) {\n                out[e] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            }\n            // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < d.blockRows(g); ++r) {\n                out[r * n + d.blockStart(g) + r] = 0;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (size_t e = 0; e < count; ++e) {\n                (void)rand_r(&seed);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const Distribution& d) {\n    // path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    for (size_t g = d.rank; g < d.nb; g += d.nprocs) {\n        const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < d.blockRows(g); ++r) {\n            const unsigned int i = static_cast<unsigned int>(d.blockStart(g) + r);\n            std::fill_n(path.data() + (off + r) * n, n, i);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax one row against pivot row k: row[j] = min(row[j], dik + rowK[j])\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict dRow, unsigned int* __restrict pRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const unsigned int dik,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dik + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = dRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        dRow[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        pRow[j] = better ? k : pRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply pivots k0..k0+nk-1 (pivot rows in buf) in order to rows [row0, row0+rows) of\n// the local matrices. Per element this is exactly the classic k-ordered update.\nstatic void applyPanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const unsigned int* buf,\n                       const size_t k0, const size_t nk, const size_t row0,\n                       const size_t rows, const size_t n, MPI_Request* pending) {\n    for (size_t r = row0; r < row0 + rows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* dRow = dist + r * n;\n        unsigned int* pRow = path + r * n;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n            relaxRow(dRow, pRow, buf + kk * n, dRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), n);\n        }\n        if (pending && *pending != MPI_REQUEST_NULL) {\n            int flag;\n            MPI_Test(pending, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);  // drive progress\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Owner of block g runs the classic algorithm over pivots of block g restricted to\n// the block's own rows, recording each pivot row at its iteration into buf.\nstatic void computePanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, unsigned int* buf,\n                         const Distribution& d, const size_t g) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n    const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n    const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n        const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n        unsigned int* rowK = buf + kk * n;\n        std::memcpy(rowK, dist + (off + kk) * n, n * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < nk; ++r) {\n            unsigned int* dRow = dist + (off + r) * n;\n            relaxRow(dRow, path + (off + r) * n, rowK, dRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), n);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const Distribution& d) {\n    // Blocked-pivot Floyd-Warshall over a block-cyclic row distribution.\n    // Pivot rows k are unchanged during iteration k (non-negative weights, zero\n    // diagonal), so each pivot block is computed by its owner and broadcast; the\n    // next panel is computed early and its broadcast overlapped with local updates.\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t B = d.B;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> bufs[2] = {std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n),\n                                         std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n)};\n    MPI_Request reqs[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    auto startBcast = [&](size_t g) {\n        unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        if (d.owner(g) == d.rank) {\n            computePanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, d, g);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf, static_cast<int>(d.blockRows(g) * n), MPI_UNSIGNED, d.owner(g),\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[g & 1]);\n    };\n\n    if (d.nb > 0) startBcast(0);\n\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        MPI_Wait(&reqs[g & 1], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n        const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n        const bool haveNext = g + 1 < d.nb;\n        const bool ownNext = haveNext && d.owner(g + 1) == d.rank;\n\n        if (haveNext) {\n            if (ownNext) {\n                applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(g + 1),\n                           d.blockRows(g + 1), n, nullptr);\n            }\n            startBcast(g + 1);\n        }\n\n        MPI_Request* pending = haveNext ? &reqs[(g + 1) & 1] : nullptr;\n        for (size_t h = d.rank; h < d.nb; h += d.nprocs) {\n            if (h == g || (ownNext && h == g + 1)) continue;\n            applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(h),\n                       d.blockRows(h), n, pending);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Collect the distributed matrix on rank 0 (full matrix returned on rank 0 only).\nstd::vector<unsigned int> gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,\n                                       const Distribution& d) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> full;\n    if (d.rank == 0) full.resize(n * n);\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        const int src = d.owner(g);\n        const size_t count = d.blockRows(g) * n;\n        if (d.rank == 0) {\n            unsigned int* dst = full.data() + d.blockStart(g) * n;\n            if (src == 0) {\n                std::memcpy(dst, local.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n, count * sizeof(unsigned int));\n            } else {\n                MPI_Recv(dst, static_cast<int>(count), MPI_UNSIGNED, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            }\n        } else if (src == d.rank) {\n            MPI_Send(local.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n, static_cast<int>(count), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                     0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    return full;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nmstart=src.index('int main(')\nmain=r'''int main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n    const bool root = rank == 0;\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Choose pivot block size: large enough for cache reuse, small enough that\n    // there are several blocks per rank for load balance and a short critical path.\n    Distribution d;\n    d.n = numNodes;\n    d.rank = rank;\n    d.nprocs = nprocs;\n    d.B = std::clamp<size_t>(numNodes / (4 * static_cast<size_t>(nprocs)), 1, 64);\n    d.nb = (numNodes + d.B - 1) / d.B;\n    d.localRows = 0;\n    for (size_t g = d);\n    \n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (root) printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    floydWarshall(dist, path, d);\n    \n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n    if (printResults || validate) fullDist = gatherMatrix(dist, d);\n    \n    // Print results for          // rows per block\n    size_t nb;         // number of global blocks\n    int rank;\n    int nprocs;\n    size_t localRows;  // number of rows stored on this rank\n\n    int owner(size_t g) const { return static_cast<int>(g % nprocs); }\n    size_t blockStart(size_t g) const { return g * B; }\n    size_t blockRows(size_t g) const { return std::min(B, n - g * B); }\n    // Local row offset of a block owned by this rank\n    size_t localOffset(size_t g) const { return (g / nprocs) * B; }\n};\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const Distribution& d,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n\n    // Replay the sequential random stream, keeping only rows owned by this rank\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        const size_t count = d.blockRows(g) * n;\n        if (d.owner(g) == d.rank) {\n            unsigned int* out = dist.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n;\n            for (size_t e = 0; e < count; ++e) {\n                out[e] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            }\n            // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < d.blockRows(g); ++r) {\n                out[r * n + d.blockStart(g) + r] = 0;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (size_t e = 0; e < count; ++e) {\n                (void)rand_r(&seed);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const Distribution& d) {\n    // Equivalent to the original initialization: path[i][j] = i\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    for (size_t g = d.rank; g < d.nb; g += d.nprocs) {\n        const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < d.blockRows(g); ++r) {\n            const unsigned int i = static_cast<unsigned int>(d.blockStart(g) + r);\n            std::fill_n(path.data() + (off + r) * n, n, i);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax one row i against pivot row k: dist[i][j] = min(dist[i][j], dist[i][k] + dist[k][j])\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict dRow, unsigned int* __restrict pRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const unsigned int dik,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dik + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = dRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        dRow[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        pRow[j] = better ? k : pRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply pivots k0..k0+nk-1 (pivot rows in buf) in order to local rows [row0, row0+rows).\n// Per element this is exactly the classic k-ordered update.\nstatic void applyPanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const unsigned int* buf,\n                       const size_t k0, const size_t nk, const size_t row0,\n                       const size_t rows, const size_t n, MPI_Request* pending) {\n    for (size_t r = row0; r < row0 + rows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* dRow = dist + r * n;\n        unsigned int* pRow = path + r * n;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n            relaxRow(dRow, pRow, buf + kk * n, dRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), n);\n        }\n        if (pending && *pending != MPI_REQUEST_NULL) {\n            int flag;\n            MPI_Test(pending, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);  // drive progress of the broadcast\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Owner of block g runs the classic algorithm over the pivots of block g restricted to\n// the block's own rows, recording each pivot row as of its iteration into buf.\nstatic void computePanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, unsigned int* buf,\n                         const Distribution& d, const size_t g) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n    const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n    const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n        const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n        unsigned int* rowK = buf + kk * n;\n        std::memcpy(rowK, dist + (off + kk) * n, n * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        for (size_t r = 0; The next pivot block\n    // is computed early and its broadcast overlaps with the local updates.\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t B = d.B;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> bufs[2] = {std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n),\n                                         std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n)};\n    MPI_Request reqs[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    auto startBcast = [&](size_t g) {\n        unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        if (d.owner(g) == d.rank) {\n            computePanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, d, g);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf, static_cast<int>(d.blockRows(g) * n), MPI_UNSIGNED, d.owner(g),\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[g & 1]);\n    };\n\n    if (d.nb > 0) startBcast(0);\n\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        MPI_Wait(&reqs[g & 1], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n        const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n        const bool haveNext = g + 1 < d.nb;\n        const bool ownNext = haveNext && d.owner(g + 1) == d.rank;\n\n        if (haveNext) {\n            if (ownNext) {\n                applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(g + 1),\n                           d.blockRows(g + 1), n, nullptr);\n            }\n            startBcast(g + 1);\n        }\n\n        MPI_Request* pending = haveNext ? &reqs[(g + 1) & 1] : nullptr;\n        for (size_t h = d.rank; h < d.nb; h += d.nprocs) {\n            if (h == g || (ownNext && h == g + 1)) continue;\n            applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(h),\n                       d.blockRows(h), n, pending);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Collect the distributed matrix on rank 0 (returned non-empty on rank 0 only)\nstd::vector<unsigned int> gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,\n                                       const Distribution& d) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> full;\n    if (d.rank == 0) full.resize(n * n);\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        const int src = d.owner(g);\n        const size_t count = d.blockRows(g) * n;\n        if (d.rank == 0) {\n            unsigned int* dst = full.data() + d.blockStart(g) * n;\n            if (src == 0) {\n                std::memcpy(dst, local.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n, count * sizeof(unsigned int));\n            } else {\n                MPI_Recv(dst, static_cast<int>(count), MPI_UNSIGNED, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            }\n        } else if (src == d.rank) {\n            MPI_Send(local.data() + d.localOffset(g) * n, static_cast<int>(count), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                     0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    return full;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD,      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Pivot block size: large enough for cache reuse of each row, small enough to give\n    // several blocks per rank (load balance) and a short serial panel critical path.\n    Distribution d;\n    d.n = numNodes;\n    d.rank = rank;\n    d.nprocs = nprocs;\n    d.B = MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n    if          // rows per block\n    size_t nb;         // number of global blocks\n    int rank;\n    int nprocs;\n    size_t localRows;  // number of rows stored on this rank\n\n    int owner(size_t g) const { return static_cast<int>(g % nprocs); }\n};\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const Distribution& d,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n\n    // Replay the sequential random stream, keeping only rows owned by this rank\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; g += d.nprocs) {\n        const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < d.blockRows(g); ++r) {\n            const unsigned int i = static_cast<unsigned int>(d.blockStart(g) + r);\n            std::fill_n(path.data() + (off + r) * n, n, i);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax one row i against pivot row k: dist[i][j] = min(dist[i][j], dist[i][k] + dist[k][j])\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict dRow, unsigned int* __restrict pRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const unsigned int dik,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dik + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = dRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        dRow[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        pRow[j] = better ? k : pRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply pivots k0..k0+nk-1 (pivot rows in buf) in order to local rows [row0, row0+rows).\n// Per element this is exactly the classic k-ordered update.\nstatic void applyPanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const unsigned int* buf,\n                       const size_t k0, const size_t nk, const size_t row0,\n                       const size_t rows, const size_t n, MPI_Request* pending) {\n    for (size_t r = row0; r < row0 + rows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* dRow = dist + r * n;\n        unsigned int* pRow = path + r * n;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n            relaxRow(dRow, pRow, buf + kk * n, dRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), n);\n        }\n        if (pending && *pending != MPI_REQUEST_NULL) {\n            int flag;\n            MPI_Test(pending, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);  // drive progress of the broadcast\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Owner of block g runs the classic algorithm over the pivots of block g restricted to\n// the block's own rows, recording each pivot row as of its iteration into buf.\nstatic void computePanel(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, unsigned int* buf,\n                         const Distribution& d, const size_t g) {\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n    const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n    const size_t off = d.localOffset(g);\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nk; ++kk) {\n        const size_t k = k0 + kk;\n        unsigned int* rowK = buf + kk * n;\n        std::memcpy(rowK, dist + (off + kk) * n, n * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        for (size_t r = 0; The next pivot block\n    // is computed early and its broadcast overlaps with the local updates.\n    const size_t n = d.n;\n    const size_t B = d.B;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> bufs[2] = {std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n),\n                                         std::vector<unsigned int>(B * n)};\n    MPI_Request reqs[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    auto startBcast = [&](size_t g) {\n        unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        if (d.owner(g) == d.rank) {\n            computePanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, d, g);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf, static_cast<int>(d.blockRows(g) * n), MPI_UNSIGNED, d.owner(g),\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[g & 1]);\n    };\n\n    if (d.nb > 0) startBcast(0);\n\n    for (size_t g = 0; g < d.nb; ++g) {\n        MPI_Wait(&reqs[g & 1], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* buf = bufs[g & 1].data();\n        const size_t k0 = d.blockStart(g);\n        const size_t nk = d.blockRows(g);\n        const bool haveNext = g + 1 < d.nb;\n        const bool ownNext = haveNext && d.owner(g + 1) == d.rank;\n\n        if (haveNext) {\n            if (ownNext) {\n                applyPanel(dist.data(), path.data(), buf, k0, nk, d.localOffset(g + 1),\n                           d.blockRows(g + 1), n, nullptr);\n            }\n            startBcast(g + 1);\n        }\n\n        MPI_Request* pending = haveNext ? Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CffheQT6eyBdTzjPDpXt2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've parallelized `floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp` with MPI. Every run produced exactly the same distance-matrix hash as the original program and passed `-v` validation. I tested 1, 3, 8, 32 and 64 ranks with n = 7, 512, 1000 and 2048.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Data split:** the rows of the matrix are dealt out to the ranks in blocks, round-robin, so each rank only stores its own rows of `dist` and `path`.\n- **Same update order:** within each row, the shortest-path updates are applied in exactly the original order, using the same strict `<` comparison. So `path` comes out identical too, not just `dist`.\n- **Pivot rows:** a row isn't modified during the step that uses it as the pivot, so its owner can compute a group of pivot rows (up to 64 at a time) and broadcast them to everyone. Each rank then applies the whole group to every row it holds while that row is still in cache.\n- **Overlap:** the next group of pivot rows is computed early and broadcast in the background while ranks do their local work, so communication mostly hides behind computation.\n- **Group size:** it shrinks automatically as you add ranks, which keeps the work balanced and the serial part short.\n- **Inner loop:** rewritten without branches so the compiler can vectorize it. That alone made one rank about 3× faster than the original (2.9 s vs 9.4 s at n=2048).\n- **Setup and output:** each rank generates its own rows by replaying the original random-number sequence, so the input matrix is identical. The full matrix is only collected on rank 0 when `-r` or `-v` is given, and that happens after the timed section. Only rank 0 prints, and every rank exits with the same validation code.\n\n**Timings (n=2048, all ranks on this one machine)**\n\n| Ranks | Time |\n|---|---|\n| Original (serial) | 9.4 s |\n| 1 | 2.9 s |\n| 8 | 0.39 s |\n| 32 | 0.21 s |\n| 64 | 0.095 s |\n\nAt n=4096 it took 0.85 s on 64 ranks and 0.39 s on 128. At the default n=512, 64 ranks was slower than 8 (13 ms vs 5 ms), because the problem is too small to share out that far. I haven't run it across several machines; these numbers are all from one 256-core box.\n\n**CMake:** `CMakeLists.txt` now uses `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`. Every run produced exactly the same distance-matrix hash as the original program and passed `-v` validation. I tested 1, 3, 8, 32 and 64 ranks with n = 7, 512, 1000 and 2048.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Data split:** the rows of the matrix are dealt out to the ranks in blocks, round-robin, so each rank only stores its own rows of `dist` and `path`.\n- **Same update order:** within each row, the shortest-path updates are applied in exactly the original order, using the same strict `<` comparison. So `path` comes out identical too, not just `dist`.\n- **Pivot rows:** a row isn't modified during the step that uses it as the pivot, so its owner can compute a group of pivot rows (up to 64 at a time) and broadcast them to everyone. Each rank then applies the whole group to every row it holds while that row is still in cache.\n- **Overlap:** the next group of pivot rows is computed early and broadcast in the background while ranks do their local work, so communication mostly hides behind computation.\n- **Group size:** it shrinks automatically as you add ranks, which keeps the work balanced and the serial part short.\n- **Inner loop:** rewritten without branches so the compiler can vectorize it. That alone made one rank about 3× faster than the original (2.9 s vs 9.4 s at n=2048).\n- **Setup and output:** each rank generates its own rows by replaying the original random-number sequence, so the input matrix is identical. The full matrix is only collected on rank 0 when `-r` or `-v` is given, and that happens after the timed section. Only rank 0 prints, and every rank exits with the same validation code.\n\n**Timings (n=2048, all ranks on this one machine)**\n\n| Ranks | Time |\n|---|---|\n| Original (serial) | 9.4 s |\n| 1 | 2.9 s |\n| 8 | 0.39 s |\n| 32 | 0.21 s |\n| 64 | 0.095 s |\n\nAt n=4096 it took 0.85 s on 64 ranks and 0.39 s on 128. At the default n=512, 64 ranks was slower than 8 (13 ms vs 5 ms), because the problem is too small to share out that far. I haven't run it across several machines; these numbers are all from one 256-core box.\n\n**CMake:** `CMakeLists.txt` now uses `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`. The executable is still called `floydwarshall` and needs no new dependencies.
